&lt;project_context>
# 项目: symb_invariant_consensus

## 运行环境
- 服务器名: westd-25068
- SSH 连接命令: ssh connect.westd.seetacloud.com  # 必须用此命令，~/.ssh/config 自动应用密钥和跳板机，禁止用裸 IP
- 项目代码根目录: /root/symb_invariant_consensus


## 环境搭建
连接服务器后先调 `detect_image` 检测是否为预置镜像。如果匹配，跳过基础环境搭建，只装项目特有依赖。已预装的包不要重复安装。

## 输出风格
回复简洁直接。只输出执行结果和必要信息，不要解释过程、不要客套、不要给未被要求的建议。
&lt;/project_context>
## 任务：检查 exp-027/028/030 状态并修复

### 服务器: westd-25068

检查 3 个实验的进程状态，修复 registry，必要时重启。

| exp_id | PID | GPU | Port |
|--------|-----|-----|------|
| exp-027-qwen3-14b-non-thinking | 38106 | cuda:1 | 8001 |
| exp-028-qwen3-14b-thinking | 40990 | cuda:2 | 8002 |
| exp-030-crossmodel-mistral-folio204 | 46525 | cuda:0 | 8000 |

### Step 1: 检查所有进程
```bash
for pid in 38106 40990 46525; do
  ps -p $pid -o pid,cmd --no-headers 2>/dev/null && echo "PID $pid ALIVE" || echo "PID $pid DEAD"
done

nvidia-smi
tmux ls 2>/dev/null
```

### Step 2: 检查日志
```bash
echo "=== exp-027 ==="
tail -30 ~/runs/symb_invariant_consensus/exp-027-qwen3-14b-non-thinking.log 2>/dev/null
echo "=== exp-028 ==="
tail -30 ~/runs/symb_invariant_consensus/exp-028-qwen3-14b-thinking.log 2>/dev/null
echo "=== exp-030 ==="
tail -30 ~/runs/symb_invariant_consensus/exp-030-crossmodel-mistral-folio204.log 2>/dev/null
```

### Step 3: 检查 vLLM
```bash
curl -s http://localhost:8000/v1/models 2>/dev/null | head -5 && echo "port 8000 OK" || echo "port 8000 DOWN"
curl -s http://localhost:8001/v1/models 2>/dev/null | head -5 && echo "port 8001 OK" || echo "port 8001 DOWN"
curl -s http://localhost:8002/v1/models 2>/dev/null | head -5 && echo "port 8002 OK" || echo "port 8002 DOWN"
```

### Step 4: 检查结果进度
```bash
echo "=== exp-027 results ==="
find /root -path "*exp-027*" -name "*.json" 2>/dev/null | head -5
find /root -path "*qwen3*non*think*" -name "*.json" 2>/dev/null | head -5
echo "=== exp-028 results ==="
find /root -path "*exp-028*" -name "*.json" 2>/dev/null | head -5
find /root -path "*qwen3*think*" -name "*.json" -not -path "*non*" 2>/dev/null | head -5
echo "=== exp-030 results ==="
find /root -path "*exp-030*" -name "*.json" 2>/dev/null | head -5
find /root -path "*cross*model*" -name "*.json" 2>/dev/null | head -5
find /root -path "*mistral*folio*" -name "*.json" 2>/dev/null | head -5
```

### Step 5: 修复 registry
- 如果进程 ALIVE 且在跑 → update status to RUNNING
- 如果进程 DEAD 且有结果 → update status to DONE + conclusion
- 如果进程 DEAD 且无结果 → update conclusion=failed + 记录原因

对每个实验做相应 update_registry。

### 产出
每个实验一行状态报告：进程状态、GPU占用、进度、日志最后几行关键信息。

Also use for: permissions (\"allow X\", \"add permission\", \"move permission to\"), env vars (\"set X=Y\"), hook troubleshooting, or any changes to settings.json/settings.local.json files. Examples: \"allow npm commands\", \"add bq permission to global settings\", \"move permission to user settings\", \"set DEBUG=true\", \"when claude stops show X\". For simple settings like theme/model, suggest the /config command.\n- keybindings-help: Use when the user wants to customize keyboard shortcuts, rebind keys, add chord bindings, or modify ~/.claude/keybindings.json. Examples: \"rebind ctrl+s\", \"add a chord shortcut\", \"change the submit key\", \"customize keybindings\".\n- simplify: Review changed code for reuse, quality, and efficiency, then fix any issues found.\n- fewer-permission-prompts: Scan your transcripts for common read-only Bash and MCP tool calls, then add a prioritized allowlist to project .claude/settings.json to reduce permission prompts.\n- loop: Run a prompt or slash command on a recurring interval (e.g. /loop 5m /foo).
